# Graph Report - ConjuntOS  (2026-08-20)

## Corpus Check
- 19568 files · ~16,431,109 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 61424 nodes · 81654 edges · 11317 communities (7707 shown, 3610 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 100% EXTRACTED · 0% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 273 edges (avg confidence: 0.79)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `b698d401`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).

Cohesion: 0.04

            mpl::and_<
                // A proxy is only needed for readable iterators
                is_convertible<
                    Reference
                    // Use add_lvalue_reference to form `reference to Value` due to
                    // some (strict) C++03 compilers (e.g. `gcc -std=c++03`) reject
                    // 'reference-to-reference' in the template which described in CWG
                    // DR106.
                    // http://www.open-std.org/Jtc1/sc22/wg21/docs/cwg_defects.html#106
                  , typename add_lvalue_reference<Value const>::type
                >

                // No multipass iterator can have values that disappear
                // before positions can be re-visited
              , mpl::not_<
                    is_convertible<
                        typename iterator_category_to_traversal<CategoryOrTraversal>::type
                      , forward_traversal_tag
                    >
                >
            >, identity<Iterator>, if_<
                is_non_proxy_reference<Reference,Value>
              , postfix_increment_proxy<Iterator>
              , writable_postfix_increment_proxy<Iterator>
